# P / DeckShell 适配：feat: remove qwlroots dependency, use wlroots C API directly

- Treeland 来源：` 9582881ddc2e71c73f01eb1f8f5efd0b87a8b107 `。
- 本仓目标：` d3726e904dac69ac8d2e5f319c2d7f076b645e23 `。
- 原同步目标：` c65523a80d93d7fb885ac16b4338169eaab99c7c `；本页文件名采用整理后的完整目标 SHA。
- 节点：N5；action=adapted。
- [本仓总记录](../summary.md#entry-101)。

## 比较口径

比较来源唯一父提交 → 来源提交，与原目标唯一父提交 → 原目标提交的逐路径增量。
零上下文补丁对照会呈现基线位置、既有命名和本次实现差异；它不是整棵来源树与目标树的长期差异，也不声称所有显示行都是本次新增适配。
改写后的普通文件与原目标相同；派生 gitlink 在总记录单列，不混进源码适配。原验收只适用于总记录列出的原节点候选。

## 已审说明

以下保留原证据文字；其中依赖 SHA 属于原运行，新依赖身份见下文与总记录。

- ` 完成原生 API 和测试调用方迁移，保留本地多座席、生命周期和 QML URI；在精确批准的既有运行时安装入口安装真实原生共享库，不借用系统或构建树库，也不增加兼容别名。 `
- ` Includes waylib-shared update to 93d893187e4bf4491a089d567e31a7a7da532c00. `
- ` 此次包含了 DeckShell/3rdparty/waylib-shared/ 的更新。 `

--- 来源的本次增量

--    # Still vendor wlroots for treeland modules that link Wlroots::wlroots.
--    add_subdirectory(wlroots)
-+    # Consume the wlroots library exported by the installed Waylib package
-+    # instead of building a second vendored copy (dual-link/ODR risk).
-@@ -77,0 +77,12 @@ else()
-+    if(NOT TARGET Wlroots::wlroots)
-+        if(TARGET Waylib::waylib-wlroots)
-+            add_library(Wlroots::wlroots ALIAS Waylib::waylib-wlroots)
-+        else()
-+            pkg_check_modules(WLR_INSTALLED REQUIRED IMPORTED_TARGET waylib-wlroots)
-+            add_library(Wlroots::wlroots ALIAS PkgConfig::WLR_INSTALLED)
-+        endif()
-+    endif()
-+    # The capture module generates protocol code with ws_generate(); load
-+    # the vendored helpers so external builds do not depend on the scanner
-+    # helpers being exported by the installed Waylib package.
-+    include(${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/waylib/cmake/WaylandScannerHelpers.cmake)
+@@ -104,9 +104,3 @@ list(APPEND LOCAL_QML_IMPORT_PATH "${PROJECT_BINARY_DIR}/waylib/src/server")
+-# if(WITH_SUBMODULE_WAYLIB)

+-#   include(
+-#     ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/qwlroots/cmake/WaylandScannerHelpers.cmake)
+-#   add_subdirectory(waylib)

+-# else()
+-#   find_package(Waylib REQUIRED Server)
+-# endif()
++# Waylib/native wlroots are provided once by the top-level waylib-shared entry.
++# Protocol consumers use Waylib's own helpers after qwlroots retirement.
++include(${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/../3rdparty/waylib-shared/waylib/cmake/WaylandScannerHelpers.cmake)
